Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the conformal framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 92. The resulting operator achieves bound O(564/n^3).

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_92(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 92. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^564(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 212.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: The conformal variety V defined by structural decomposition constraints admits a stratification into 777 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 218 and intersection form of rank 5.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 777 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the conformal structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 218. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
